_nghost-imotionsserverapp-c7="" headertitle="h3"><div _ngcontent-imotionsserverapp-c7="" class="module-align-center"><!-- --><!-- --><!-- --><h3 _ngcontent-imotionsserverapp-c7=""><span style="color: #fac011;">Unpack Human Behavior</span>
</h3><!-- --><!-- --><h4 _ngcontent-imotionsserverapp-c7=""><span style="font-weight: 300; color: #efefef;">iMotions helps you quantify engagement and emotional responses</span>
</h4><!-- --><!-- --><!-- --><!-- --><!-- --><div _ngcontent-imotionsserverapp-c7="" class="organization-category-wrap"><!-- --></div><!-- --><!-- --><!-- --><!-- --><div _ngcontent-imotionsserverapp-c7="" class="module-description"><p><span style="font-weight: 300;">Emotions constitute powerful, pervasive, and predictable drivers of decision making. </span>Capture physiological reactions in real time to accurately research how we as humans think, feel, act and respond.<br/>
Beyond what we say we do.</p>

headertitle="h3"><div _ngcontent-imotionsserverapp-c7="" class="module-align-left align-left"><!-- --><!-- --><!-- --><h3 _ngcontent-imotionsserverapp-c7="">The iMotions Platform
</h3><!-- --><!-- --><h4 _ngcontent-imotionsserverapp-c7=""><span style="color: #fac011;">Gain a holistic view of human behavior</span>
</h4><!-- --><!-- --><!-- --><!-- --><!-- --><div _ngcontent-imotionsserverapp-c7="" class="organization-category-wrap"><!-- --></div><!-- --><!-- --><!-- --><!-- --><div _ngcontent-imotionsserverapp-c7="" class="module-description"><p><span style="font-weight: 300;">iMotions is high tech software made to execute human behavior research with high validity. iMotions seamlessly integrates multiple biosensors that provide different insight; such as Eye Tracking, EDA/GSR, EEG, ECG and Facial Expression Analysis. The combination of different sensors and data sources allows you to make a clearer and more incisive understanding of human behavior. Through real-time measurements of nonconcious responses, iMotions provides the bigger picture on human actions, thoughts and feelings for you to tap into new innovation – getting you the results faster. </span></p>
<ul>
<li>Hardware agnostic software with +50 biosensor devices</li>
<li>Seamless integration of even your own research data</li>
<li>Full scalability when you need it</li>
<li>Accelerated output for validated and fast results</li>
</ul>

It houses the Emotion and Social Relations Lab within the Department of Experimental Psychology, in which Dr. Danielle Shore works to understand how social information impacts social interactions. &l;/span&g;&l;/p&g;\n&l;p&g;&l;span style=\&q;font-weight: 400;\&q;&g;This is achieved by examining how facial expressions within social interactions influence decision making processes. style=\&q;font-weight: 400;\&q;&g;Dr. Danielle Shore uses Affectiva’s facial expression analysis within iMotions in combination with Game Theory experiments to examine how facial expressions can influence decision making outcomes in interactive settings. The non-invasive nature of facial expression analysis through a webcam allows for the collection of natural, ecologically valid data which is important for gathering an accurate understanding of emotional expressions. As Danielle states, &a;#8220;one of the really wonderful things we can do with iMotions is we can actually look at these expressions as they occur in time, live, and actually record that data and decode what&a;#8217;s going on in the face in an ongoing interactive environment&a;#8221;. Furthermore, the computational and automatic analysis of facial expressions reduces the workload required, and increases the speed at which results can be collected, allowing for a quicker understanding of participant reactions.&l;/span&g;&l;/p&g;\n&l;p&g;&a;nbsp;&l;/p&g;\n&q;,&q;text_align&q;:&q;align-left&q;},{&q;blurb_icon&q;:{&q;ID&q;:25268,&q;id&q;:25268,&q;title&q;:&q;icon The modular nature of the iMotions software also provides the opportunity to expand in the future if desired, without the need to learn new systems, while also allowing continuous data compatibility. As Danielle recalls, &a;#8220;when I was looking at the different options that were available it seemed to be the platform that was going to be best be adapted to suit the needs of the lab, especially as we foresee them changing over time&a;#8221;.&l;/span&g;&l;/p&g;\n&l;p&g;&l;span style=\&q;font-weight: 400;\&q;&g;Affectiva’s facial expression analysis algorithm allows new questions within the behavioral communications agency in London, HeyHuman thinks differently about people, brands, and marketing. Traditional advertising tactics and metrics are outdated; people are overloaded in social and digital content and it&a;#8217;s hard to parse out how people are &l;em&g;actually &l;/em&g;engaging with content. Aoife McGuinness, Neuroscience Consultant and head of their neuroscience research lab, flips the script by helping brands nurture a new kind of relationship with their customers &a;#8211; through focusing on their behavior, not their words.&l;/p&g;\n&l;p&g;&a;#8220;I think what people say and what people do are often not the same, and I think we recognize the importance of tapping into this unconscious response to get real insight that&a;#8217;s going to deliver effective results,&a;#8221; she says.&l;/p&g;\n&q;,&q;text_align&q;:&q;align-left&q;},{&q;blurb_icon&q;:{&q;ID&q;:25279,&q;id&q;:25279,&q;title&q;:&q;icon allows Aoife and team to develop ways to understand how people are really engaging with content, down to the cognitive processes going on underneath the surface. HeyHuman&a;#8217;s approach with biosensors focuses on EEG and eye tracking for studies across advertising, UX testing, concept development, and guiding strategic ideas. Testing using &l;a href=\&q;/biosensors/\&q;&g;these sensors&l;/a&g; (an ABM B-Alert EEG headset, and a Tobii eye tracker) helps them work through different hypotheses about their audiences. They can see how people are really reacting to their content by measuring engagement, recall, cognitive overload, and physiological animation_offset=\&q;\&q; class=\&q;\&q; id=\&q;\&q;][fusion_text]\r\n&l;h3&g;Highlights&l;/h3&g;\r\n&l;ul&g;\r\n \t&l;li&g;Biosensors provide a better understanding of human behavior through neuroeconomics, including how emotions drive behavior and self-control&l;/li&g;\r\n \t&l;li&g;Engaging in cutting-edge research is possible thanks to these tools&l;/li&g;\r\n \t&l;li&g;Multiple signals help show \&q;the whole picture\&q; of human behavior&l;/li&g;\r\n \t&l;li&g;Alleviated worries about the interaction between hardware and software&l;/li&g;\r\n&l;/ul&g;\r\n[/fusion_text][/fusion_builder_column][fusion_builder_column type=\&q;1_1\&q; background_position=\&q;left top\&q; background_color=\&q;\&q; border_size=\&q;\&q; border_color=\&q;\&q; border_style=\&q;solid\&q; spacing=\&q;yes\&q; background_image=\&q;\&q; background_repeat=\&q;no-repeat\&q; padding=\&q;\&q; margin_top=\&q;0px\&q; margin_bottom=\&q;0px\&q; min_height=\&q;none\&q;][fusion_separator style_type=\&q;single solid\&q; top_margin=\&q;10px\&q; bottom_margin=\&q;30px\&q; sep_color=\&q;#b7b7b7\&q; border_size=\&q;\&q; icon=\&q;\&q; animation_type=\&q;\&q; animation_direction=\&q;\&q; animation_speed=\&q;0.1\&q; animation_offset=\&q;\&q; class=\&q;\&q; id=\&q;\&q;][fusion_text]\r\n&l;h3&g;Challenges&l;/h3&g;\r\n[/fusion_text][fusion_text]Texas A&a;amp;M University is the flagship university of the state of Texas, with almost 69,000 attending students. In the Agriculture and Life Sciences Department, Professor Marco Palma leads the Human Behavior Lab, a specially designed facility built to advance knowledge within the field of neuroeconomics, and with human behavior research in general.\r\n\r\nBefore working with iMotions, Professor Palma didn&s;t have an objective way to measure emotions. Since 2016, however, he has quickly gone on to build a lab of 16 units installed with iMotions, with various constellations of devices for eye tracking, EEG, galvanic skin response, and facial expression analysis. He credits the combination of biosensors in iMotions with allowing the lab to build upon their prior success in collecting this otherwise unobtainable data to further their research.[/fusion_text][/fusion_builder_column][fusion_builder_column type=\&q;1_3\&q; last=\&q;no\&q; spacing=\&q;yes\&q; center_content=\&q;no\&q; hide_on_mobile=\&q;no\&q; background_color=\&q;\&q; background_image=\&q;\&q; background_repeat=\&q;no-repeat\&q; background_position=\&q;left top\&q; hover_type=\&q;none\&q; link=\&q;\&q; border_position=\&q;all\&q; border_size=\&q;0px\&q; border_color=\&q;\&q; border_style=\&q;\&q; padding=\&q;\&q; margin_top=\&q;\&q; margin_bottom=\&q;\&q; animation_type=\&q;\&q; animation_direction=\&q;\&q; animation_speed=\&q;0.1\&q; animation_offset=\&q;\&q; class=\&q;\&q; id=\&q;\&q;][fusion_text]\r\n&l;h3&g;Benefits&l;/h3&g;\r\n[/fusion_text][fusion_text]The expansion of the Human Behavior Lab has allowed the researchers to amplify the reach and depth of their investigations into human behavior, as well as to greatly scale the number of participants that they can process. The tools that they use (eye tracking, EEG, GSR, and facial expression analysis) pave the way for advancing the science of human decision-making.\r\n\r\nProfessor Palma describes in the video how the combination of sensors is a breakthrough for the field of neuroeconomics that allows researchers to get the whole picture of human behavior.[/fusion_text][/fusion_builder_column][fusion_builder_column type=\&q;1_3\&q; last=\&q;yes\&q; spacing=\&q;yes\&q; center_content=\&q;no\&q; hide_on_mobile=\&q;no\&q; background_color=\&q;\&q; background_image=\&q;\&q; background_repeat=\&q;no-repeat\&q; background_position=\&q;left top\&q; hover_type=\&q;none\&q; link=\&q;\&q; border_position=\&q;all\&q; border_size=\&q;0px\&q; border_color=\&q;\&q; border_style=\&q;\&q; padding=\&q;\&q; margin_top=\&q;\&q; margin_bottom=\&q;\&q; animation_type=\&q;\&q; animation_direction=\&q;\&q; animation_speed=\&q;0.1\&q; animation_offset=\&q;\&q; class=\&q;\&q; id=\&q;\&q;][fusion_text]\r\n&l;h3&g;Solution&l;/h3&g;\r\n[/fusion_text][fusion_text]The Human Behavior Lab represents the culmination of several years&s; work, which Professor Marco Palma has been central in leading. Over the last few years, the research group has been building onto--and showcasing the advantages of--combining sensors and iMotions, so they are now able to scale their experiments and execute research faster than ever.\r\n\r\nProfessor Marco Palma recounts how iMotions has \&q;been part of our success since the beginning\&q;, and that he \&q;looks forward to growing together in the future\&q;.[/fusion_text][/fusion_builder_column][/fusion_builder_row][/fusion_builder_container][fusion_builder_container background_color=\&q;\&q; background_image=\&q;\&q; A&a;amp;M University is the flagship university of the state of Texas, with almost 69,000 attending students. In the Agriculture and Life Sciences Department, Professor Marco Palma leads the Human Behavior Lab, a specially designed facility built to advance knowledge within the field of neuroeconomics, and with human behavior research in general.  Before working with iMotions, Professor Palma didn’t have an objective way to measure emotions. Since 2016, however, he has quickly gone on to build a lab of 16 units installed with iMotions, with various constellations of devices for eye tracking, EEG, galvanic skin response, and facial expression analysis. He credits the combination of biosensors in iMotions with allowing the lab to build upon their prior success in collecting this otherwise unobtainable data to further their research.&l;/p&g;\n&q;,&q;text_align&q;:&q;align-left&q;},{&q;blurb_icon&q;:{&q;ID&q;:24462,&q;id&q;:24462,&q;title&q;:&q;Education&q;,&q;filename&q;:&q;Education.svg&q;,&q;filesize&q;:2203,&q;url&q;:&q;https://media.imotions.com/images/20190516091711/Education.svg&q;,&q;link&q;:&q;https://publish.imotions.com/frontpage/attachment/education-2/&q;,&q;alt&q;:&q;abstract animation_type=\&q;\&q; animation_direction=\&q;\&q; animation_speed=\&q;0.1\&q; animation_offset=\&q;\&q; class=\&q;\&q; id=\&q;\&q;][fusion_text]\r\n&l;h3&g;Challenges&l;/h3&g;\r\n[/fusion_text][fusion_text]GlaxoSmithKline (GSK) is one of the world&s;s biggest pharmaceutical companies, with around 100,000 employees and yearly revenues of around £30 billion (40 billion USD). When first establishing their Shopper Science Lab, GSK sought out a flexible platform powerful enough to integrate a variety of devices and present a range of stimuli for their consumer research.\r\n\r\nCrispin Haywood, EMEA &a;amp; Americas Commercial Excellence Director, oversees operations of the Shopper Science Lab, which lives within the company&s;s consumer health business unit. The Shopper Science Lab is a cutting-edge facility designed to advance an understanding of consumer behavior. The lab provides space for both focus group research, as well as study of behavior in realistic physical shopping environments. The research aims to provide insight into pack design preferences, e-commerce behavior, positioning, and more.[/fusion_text][/fusion_builder_column][fusion_builder_column type=\&q;1_3\&q; last=\&q;no\&q; spacing=\&q;yes\&q; center_content=\&q;no\&q; hide_on_mobile=\&q;no\&q; background_color=\&q;\&q; background_image=\&q;\&q; background_repeat=\&q;no-repeat\&q; background_position=\&q;left top\&q; hover_type=\&q;none\&q; link=\&q;\&q; border_position=\&q;all\&q; border_size=\&q;0px\&q; border_color=\&q;\&q; border_style=\&q;\&q; padding=\&q;\&q; margin_top=\&q;\&q; margin_bottom=\&q;\&q; animation_type=\&q;\&q; animation_direction=\&q;\&q; animation_speed=\&q;0.1\&q; animation_offset=\&q;\&q; class=\&q;\&q; id=\&q;\&q;][fusion_text]\r\n&l;h3&g;Benefits&l;/h3&g;\r\n[/fusion_text][fusion_text]The work carried out at the Shopper Science Lab uses biometric tools to give an insight into shopper behavior. iMotions allows an integration of all of the biometric tools used within the Shopper Science Lab, and is their main software platform. The software allows them to understand shopper&s;s emotional behavior and triggers through Galvanic Skin Response (GSR), eye tracking, and facial expression analysis, along with self-reports.\r\n\r\nCrispin describes how iMotions is a \&q;360-degree offering\&q; allowing them to fully leverage the capabilities afforded at the Shopper Science Lab. The tools are used within both the stationary lab setting and in the physical shop environment (a space designed to look just like a real shop).[/fusion_text][/fusion_builder_column][fusion_builder_column type=\&q;1_3\&q; last=\&q;yes\&q; spacing=\&q;yes\&q; center_content=\&q;no\&q; hide_on_mobile=\&q;no\&q; background_color=\&q;\&q; background_image=\&q;\&q; background_repeat=\&q;no-repeat\&q; background_position=\&q;left top\&q; hover_type=\&q;none\&q; link=\&q;\&q; border_position=\&q;all\&q; border_size=\&q;0px\&q; border_color=\&q;\&q; border_style=\&q;\&q; padding=\&q;\&q; margin_top=\&q;\&q; margin_bottom=\&q;\&q; animation_type=\&q;\&q; animation_direction=\&q;\&q; animation_speed=\&q;0.1\&q; animation_offset=\&q;\&q; class=\&q;\&q; id=\&q;\&q;][fusion_text]\r\n&l;h3&g;Solution&l;/h3&g;\r\n[/fusion_text][fusion_text]By using biometric tools, the Shopper Science Lab team have been able to understand and unpack consumer behavior in greater detail. Information about emotional arousal through GSR recordings, visual attention through eye tracking, and emotional expressions through facial expression analysis, have all added valuable insights previously missing in their journey to advance shopper research.\r\n\r\nThe iMotions platform has also helped GSK elevate conversations with, and be an instrumental partner to other retail partners.[/fusion_text][/fusion_builder_column][/fusion_builder_row][/fusion_builder_container][fusion_builder_container background_color=\&q;\&q; (GSK) is one of the world’s biggest pharmaceutical companies, with around 100,000 employees and yearly revenues of around £30 billion (40 billion USD). When first establishing their Shopper Science Lab, GSK sought out a flexible platform powerful enough to integrate a variety of devices and present a range of stimuli for their consumer research.&l;/p&g;\n&l;p&g;Crispin Haywood, EMEA &a;amp; Americas Commercial Excellence Director, oversees operations of the Shopper Science Lab, which lives within the company’s consumer health business unit. The Shopper Science Lab is a cutting-edge facility designed to advance an understanding of consumer behavior. The lab provides space for both focus group research, as well as study of behavior in realistic physical shopping environments. The research aims to provide insight into pack design preferences, e-commerce behavior, positioning, and more.&l;/p&g;\n&q;,&q;text_align&q;:&q;align-left&q;},{&q;blurb_icon&q;:{&q;ID&q;:24462,&q;id&q;:24462,&q;title&q;:&q;Education&q;,&q;filename&q;:&q;Education.svg&q;,&q;filesize&q;:2203,&q;url&q;:&q;https://media.imotions.com/images/20190516091711/Education.svg&q;,&q;link&q;:&q;https://publish.imotions.com/frontpage/attachment/education-2/&q;,&q;alt&q;:&q;abstract work carried out at the Shopper Science Lab uses biometric tools to give an insight into shopper behavior. iMotions allows an integration of all of the biometric tools used within the Shopper Science Lab, and is their main software platform. The software allows them to understand shopper’s emotional behavior and triggers through Galvanic Skin Response (GSR), eye tracking, and facial expression analysis, along with self-reports.&l;/p&g;\n&l;p&g;Crispin describes how iMotions is a “360-degree offering” allowing them to fully leverage the capabilities afforded at the Shopper Science Lab. The tools are used within both the stationary lab setting and in the physical shop environment (a space designed to look just like a real shop).&l;/p&g;\n&q;,&q;text_align&q;:&q;align-left&q;},{&q;blurb_icon&q;:{&q;ID&q;:24461,&q;id&q;:24461,&q;title&q;:&q;Automotive&q;,&q;filename&q;:&q;Automotive.svg&q;,&q;filesize&q;:1687,&q;url&q;:&q;https://media.imotions.com/images/20190516091710/Automotive.svg&q;,&q;link&q;:&q;https://publish.imotions.com/frontpage/attachment/automotive/&q;,&q;alt&q;:&q;abstract automated gaze-mapping technology, in which gaze from dynamic environments is converted to static scenes for simpler aggregation and Caused by a stimulus the sweat glands become more active, increasing moisture on the skin and allowing the current to flow more readily by changing the balance of positive and negative ions in the secreted fluid (increasing skin conductance).&l;/p&g;\n&l;p&g;The Shimmer3 GSR device is compatible with the iMotions EDA / GSR
